<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Vn more</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;

}

	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {

table,

}

table {

}

th,

}

}

ol,
ul {

p > .user {

td > .user,
td > time {

input[type="checkbox"] {

}

p {

}

.image {

.code,
code {

code {

}

}

.bookmark {

.bookmark-title {

.bookmark-text {

}

	flex: 4 1 180px;

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;
	height: 16;
	background-size: 16px;
	margin-left: 2px;
	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

.checkbox-off {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20x%3D%220.75%22%20y%3D%220.75%22%20width%3D%2214.5%22%20height%3D%2214.5%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20stroke%3D%22%2336352F%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}
	
</style></head><body><article id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-9d8e-e8df6ade918a" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Vn more</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a6c1-e00e9c58b955" class="">Dưới đây là <strong>tập 1–100</strong> theo đúng format bạn đang ép: <strong>mỗi tín hiệu = 2 micro-equations (A/B)</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-a1de-cc23eff12ed7" class="">(Chấm 0–1 cho biến quan sát là đủ.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-a3c9-f78d27bf2008"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-bcea-e9febd3d0ea5" class="">1–10 (Kỷ luật nền / không gian công cộng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a7a4-fc06beebe6ac" class=""><strong>1A</strong>: Queue_integrity (Q) = 1 − Cut_in_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-95b5-f561fafa67c2" class=""><strong>1B</strong>: Trust (T) = T0 + t1·Q</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b10a-ef928debc997" class=""><strong>2A</strong>: Litter_rate = Trash_seen / Distance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b179-dff61164d55e" class=""><strong>2B</strong>: Public_care = pc0 − pc1·Litter_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-96d6-fc5f6d3aae2c" class=""><strong>3A</strong>: Noise_floor = Avg_dB_night (0–1 chuẩn hoá)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-9f27-fe1db3194424" class=""><strong>3B</strong>: Autonomic_load_pop = al0 + al1·Noise_floor</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-8093-d9055136c123" class=""><strong>4A</strong>: Sidewalk_capture = Encroachment_length / Total_length</p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-adb2-ea46df07f5f1" class=""><strong>4B</strong>: Rule_respect = rr0 − rr1·Sidewalk_capture</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-a15d-f7dd0e7172f8" class=""><strong>5A</strong>: Traffic_micro_violation = Helmets_off + Red_light_creep + Wrong_lane</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-8a59-da0c4932523b" class=""><strong>5B</strong>: Enforcement_needed = en0 + en1·Traffic_micro_violation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b307-d770d91753e9" class=""><strong>6A</strong>: Public_space_predictability = 1 − Surprise_obstacle_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-acdc-d0256720c9e6" class=""><strong>6B</strong>: Stress_baseline = sb0 + sb1·(1−Public_space_predictability)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-9619-eff883868334" class=""><strong>7A</strong>: “Nhờ vả hệ thống” rate = Favor_requests / Service_requests</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a2bd-ec8260f68d69" class=""><strong>7B</strong>: Network_dependence (N) = n0 + n1·Favor_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-a677-ca1226b214ee" class=""><strong>8A</strong>: Complaint_visibility = Public_complaints / Private_complaints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-973a-c892ef3429d5" class=""><strong>8B</strong>: Psychological_safety = ps0 + ps1·Complaint_visibility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-87c0-ce31889e949f" class=""><strong>9A</strong>: Hygiene_variance = Var(cleanliness across blocks)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b1d0-cec0468d05b3" class=""><strong>9B</strong>: Institutional_consistency = ic0 − ic1·Hygiene_variance</p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-80dc-dc1ba80105f2" class=""><strong>10A</strong>: Small_bribe_probability = σ(a1·Friction + a2·Urgency)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-87fd-e7ae59755033" class=""><strong>10B</strong>: Shadow_activity (H) = h0 + h1·Small_bribe_probability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-87c1-f9d23437e927"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-8783-e6768a3af3e8" class="">11–20 (Hành chính / giấy tờ)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-b3c3-d658ca67cf59" class=""><strong>11A</strong>: Procedure_steps = Avg_steps_observed</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-af1a-de9f0dfc3b80" class=""><strong>11B</strong>: Friction (F) = f0 + f1·Procedure_steps</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bedd-da03ea4c807b" class=""><strong>12A</strong>: Rework_rate = Returned_files / Submitted_files</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b7c5-d6db30640243" class=""><strong>12B</strong>: Clarity = c0 − c1·Rework_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-ac69-ebcab2612db2" class=""><strong>13A</strong>: “Thiếu giấy” frequency = Missing_doc_events / Case</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8fa1-d20595509aed" class=""><strong>13B</strong>: F = f0 + f2·Missing_doc_frequency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-b6bd-e27b6f6e838d" class=""><strong>14A</strong>: Time_uncertainty = Var(wait_time)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-b981-e09715f41c72" class=""><strong>14B</strong>: T = T0 − t2·Time_uncertainty</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-995c-c9fc6e8c88dc" c
lass=""><strong>15A</strong>: Informal_channel_use = “có người quen” / total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9ecf-e373847ebec9" class=""><strong>15B</strong>: Formal_system_use = 1 − Informal_channel_use</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-a994-ed58e06906ab" class=""><strong>16A</strong>: Signature_dependency = #signatures_required</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-ad42-d111b8f0aa62" class=""><strong>16B</strong>: Accountability_diffusion = ad0 + ad1·Signature_dependency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8c7c-ca5e5bd444a0" class=""><strong>17A</strong>: ID_linkage_errors = Mismatch_events / total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b523-f53153a1b274" class=""><strong>17B</strong>: Service_trust = st0 − st1·ID_linkage_errors</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-b7e5-e4863182e40c" class=""><strong>18A</strong>: Document_copy_demand = Hardcopy_required (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-b949-ef3923bbf040" class=""><strong>18B</strong>: Digital_value = dv0 − dv1·Document_copy_demand</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-91e1-c6231b47e0a2" class=""><strong>19A</strong>: “Không có hướng dẫn” rate = No_guideline_events / total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-b48d-cef3109183a8" class=""><strong>19B</strong>: Implementation_gap = ig0 + ig1·No_guideline_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-88f4-eb9151fa15e1" class=""><strong>20A</strong>: Fee_opacity = 1 − Fee_disclosed_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8987-fc0e5da1064d" class=""><strong>20B</strong>: T = T0 − t3·Fee_opacity</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a148-f0a74219689a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8784-dc83d2b94a4b" class="">21–30 (Doanh nghiệp nhỏ / hợp đồng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-a679-da52d3bb1fa7" class=""><strong>21A</strong>: Contract_enforcement = Win_case_prob (quan sát)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-b8c6-d94b034a8482" class=""><strong>21B</strong>: Oral_deal_weight = 1 − Contract_enforcement</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-ab07-e55cc706c551" class=""><strong>22A</strong>: Late_payment_norm = Late_pay_events / invoices</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-ad96-f137b77f34f0" class=""><strong>22B</strong>: Liquidity_stress = ls0 + ls1·Late_payment_norm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-a2db-cd5684e48048" class=""><strong>23A</strong>: Invoice_avoidance = Cash_only_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-bb13-e5eb2baf51e1" class=""><strong>23B</strong>: Tax_base_visibility = tv0 − tv1·Invoice_avoidance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-844e-c831022e203d" class=""><strong>24A</strong>: Warranty_real = Warranty_honored / Warranty_promised</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9640-e525486d084b" class=""><strong>24B</strong>: Consumer_trust = ct0 + ct1·Warranty_real</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8140-c47ef50b7c2d" class=""><strong>25A</strong>: Quality_variance = Var(product quality same SKU)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8df3-e326378a6210" class=""><strong>25B</strong>: Brand_trust = bt0 − bt1·Quality_variance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-94dc-e52c526b15ba" class=""><strong>26A</strong>: Supplier_switch_cost = “phải quen mới làm” (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bb00-da8a5b51cbb5" class=""><strong>26B</strong>: Competition = cp0 − cp1·Supplier_switch_cost</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-a590-c17ae51f5027" class=""><strong>27A</strong>: Compliance_when_observed (O) = Compliance_at_checkpoint</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-a92d-eaf5b7edeed5" class=""><strong>27B</strong>: T ≈ U/O (liên kết với tập trước)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-8487-dd04a82cf26f" class=""><strong>28A</strong>: Delivery_rate = On_time_deliveries / total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a0a8-d499724ccf3b" class=""><strong>28B</strong>: Trust_partner = tp0 + tp1·Delivery_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b4a5-d1e8d131d7d7" class=""><strong>29A</strong>: “Làm cho xong” bias = Patch_fix / Proper_fix</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-b886-cca5ec3786e7" class=""><strong>29B</strong>: Long_term_cost = lc0 + lc1·“Làm cho xong” bias</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8617-c73ff2caf731" class=""><strong>30A</strong>: Escalation_need = Manager_needed / cases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-a227-ff78b5543da1" class=""><strong>30B</strong>: Process_maturity = pm0 − pm1·Escalation_need</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-bd94-dbbf0987a71c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-abae-f9c3edf4086f" class="">31–40 (Thị trường lao động / năng lực)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-82dc-cf2d0dd112e5" c
lass=""><strong>31A</strong>: Credential_density = Degrees_per_role</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-819a-f643754ced47" class=""><strong>31B</strong>: Skill_density = Output_quality (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-a2d7-e208eb70f1d2" class=""><strong>32A</strong>: Training_rework = “đào tạo lại” time / total</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-aa3e-c491edd0ce84" class=""><strong>32B</strong>: Productivity = pr0 − pr1·Training_rework</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9a6e-ea4ef68e056f" class=""><strong>33A</strong>: Nepotism_signal = Hires_via_network / hires</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9350-fa544dbc880c" class=""><strong>33B</strong>: Meritocracy = m0 − m1·Nepotism_signal</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-85b6-de8750dda719" class=""><strong>34A</strong>: Fear_blame = Avoid_decision_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-93de-fa79302441bd" class=""><strong>34B</strong>: Decision_rate = dr0 − dr1·Fear_blame</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9f06-f712fe257b90" class=""><strong>35A</strong>: Micromanagement = Checkpoints_per_task</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-91c1-e6ac08b9470a" class=""><strong>35B</strong>: Autonomy = au0 − au1·Micromanagement</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-af65-ecf8ff609713" class=""><strong>36A</strong>: Staff_turnover = Leaves / headcount</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-a183-e04ce3643adf" class=""><strong>36B</strong>: Org_memory = om0 − om1·Staff_turnover</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a4e2-cb2a45171c41" c
lass=""><strong>37A</strong>: Overtime_norm = Overtime_days / days</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-9075-fb90b44c79d8" class=""><strong>37B</strong>: Burnout = bo0 + bo1·Overtime_norm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-9292-d25f945db874" class=""><strong>38A</strong>: Understaffing = Needed_roles − Filled_roles</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-97cc-cc34a985e092" class=""><strong>38B</strong>: Error_rate = er0 + er1·Understaffing</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8481-d0462f3da35b" class=""><strong>39A</strong>: Multi-tasking_pressure = Concurrent_tasks / worker</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b6c1-cedf9f288f94" class=""><strong>39B</strong>: Cognitive_load = cl0 + cl1·Multi_tasking_pressure</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9b66-d9724edc9ec6" class=""><strong>40A</strong>: Wage_to_cost = Wage / Living_cost (chuẩn hoá)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a249-f86f22136a09" class=""><strong>40B</strong>: Informal_income_need = ii0 + ii1·(1−Wage_to_cost)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-b85f-dd42839ffba4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-841f-fb56c0f0d9f1" class="">41–50 (Y tế / an sinh thực tế)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-85d5-c24528daaed8" class=""><strong>41A</strong>: Primary_care_access = 1 − Delay_to_visit</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-bd82-d8334f2025d9" class=""><strong>41B</strong>: Preventive_behavior = pb0 + pb1·Primary_care_access</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bacf-c7cdd0543cc4" class=""><strong>42A</strong>: Out_of_pocket_burden = oop0 + o
op1·(1−Coverage_effective)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-96cf-fc4d5e2033b0" class=""><strong>42B</strong>: Defensive_saving = ds0 + ds1·Out_of_pocket_burden</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-8434-fddcca4cd890" class=""><strong>43A</strong>: Pharmacy_self_med = Self_med_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-a6ce-e79a63575f13" class=""><strong>43B</strong>: Hidden_risk_health = hrh0 + hrh1·Pharmacy_self_med</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-838f-f73da0ce3391" class=""><strong>44A</strong>: Doctor_trust = dt0 − dt1·“mỗi bác sĩ nói 1 kiểu” rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-a093-ee0bf426a3f7" class=""><strong>44B</strong>: Care_continuity = cc0 + cc1·Doctor_trust</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-82e1-d25c7702c9c6" class=""><strong>45A</strong>: Diagnostics_overuse = Test_rate_without_pathway</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9dba-df3d570755e7" class=""><strong>45B</strong>: Cost_waste = cw0 + cw1·Diagnostics_overuse</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8eed-e5f0cfd62dd4" class=""><strong>46A</strong>: Follow_up_rate = Follow_up_visits / acute_visits</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-928a-fffd2369c7da" class=""><strong>46B</strong>: Chronic_control = ch0 + ch1·Follow_up_rate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9d76-c7ed455a40c6" class=""><strong>47A</strong>: ER_dependency = ER_visits / total_visits</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-885a-c68507aa8030" class=""><strong>47B</strong>: System_prevention = sp0 − sp1·ER_dependency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i

d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8727-d18d2a10c6cd" class=""><strong>86A</strong>: Social_overload = People_count·Noise·Unpredictability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9d1f-fe9d5716e115" class=""><strong>86B</strong>: Recovery_need = rn0 + rn1·Social_overload</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-b6a9-fb428ecdb447" class=""><strong>87A</strong>: Care_norm = Presence_when_sick (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-bff7-d244e17296a8" class=""><strong>87B</strong>: Relationship_trust = rt0 + rt1·Care_norm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b018-d87f300e4f37" class=""><strong>88A</strong>: “Nhờ người khác lo” = Delegation_of_care (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-add1-c4c681a42aab" class=""><strong>88B</strong>: Bond_strength = bs0 − bs1·Delegation_of_care</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-8fd4-fa74e6e1d17b" class=""><strong>89A</strong>: Integrity_consistency = Words_match_actions (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9153-f71911e31bf7" class=""><strong>89B</strong>: Safety = s0 + s1·Integrity_consistency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-a6dd-e3de4f4e83ff" class=""><strong>90A</strong>: Boundary_respect = Respect_no (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a4f8-fc15643e7c8a" class=""><strong>90B</strong>: Co_regulation_possible = cr0 + cr1·Boundary_respect</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-affd-eba7548f0314"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-8d12-da6f9baac63e" class="">91–100 (Meta cá nhân → hệ xã hội)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9dc8-c71b60854692" c
lass=""><strong>91A</strong>: Chronic_overdrive = Sympathetic_baseline (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-b77e-d2149cf17142" class=""><strong>91B</strong>: Sensory_gain = sg0 + sg1·Chronic_overdrive</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-b986-fd5e3dbd2e1d" class=""><strong>92A</strong>: Buffer_reserve = Sleep + Nutrition + Quiet (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-be22-f12cdd43de00" class=""><strong>92B</strong>: Stability = st0 + st1·Buffer_reserve</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-bb31-ca55b6b40003" class=""><strong>93A</strong>: Recovery_channel = Parasympathetic_access (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-9910-ce26c27630d9" class=""><strong>93B</strong>: Pain_amplification = pa0 + pa1·(1−Recovery_channel)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a1bb-e6737c2c5587" class=""><strong>94A</strong>: Input_filter = Thalamic_gating (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a920-d6e0d05a926e" class=""><strong>94B</strong>: Overload = ol0 + ol1·(1−Input_filter)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a901-c9ed99ec1c5b" class=""><strong>95A</strong>: Environment_alignment = Low_noise + Predictability + Autonomy</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-ba42-d800f6ffbac3" class=""><strong>95B</strong>: Regulation = rg0 + rg1·Environment_alignment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a78a-d1b52706fbc2" class=""><strong>96A</strong>: Regulation → Performance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" i

Crash\_risk = cr0 + cr2·(1−Regulation)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-9e62-e10977489636" class=""><strong>97A</strong>: Cognitive_override = Prefrontal_control (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b242-e13235c92828" class=""><strong>97B</strong>: Hidden_cost = hc0 + hc2·Cognitive_override·(1−Regulation)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-8343-d6e2fa2f182f" class=""><strong>98A</strong>: Integrity_load = Truth_cost_in_field (0–1)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-87db-dba7aae6e36a" class=""><strong>98B</strong>: Social_friction = sf0 + sf1·Integrity_load·(1−T)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-9220-c2e8372fc4dd" class=""><strong>99A</strong>: System_damping = dp0 − dp2·Ambient_chaos</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-aa39-e432278dad47" class=""><strong>99B</strong>: Gain = g0 + g6·(1−System_damping)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9c0b-e27502b933aa" class=""><strong>100A</strong>: Net_system_health = T + Delivery − Shadow − Friction</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8f63-ed39f81184e3" class=""><strong>100B</strong>: If Net_system_health &lt;

partition=true \Rightarrow \text{consistency model declared}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-a646-edc4ad5524f0" class=""><strong>410. Split-brain forbidden</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-a2ca-fbee3f7a7b5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\text{active leaders}(t)|\le 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b494-d654960768dc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b71e-e5f9982544cb" class="">B) CRDT / merge invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-934e-c84bba4cf611" class=""><strong>411. Commutativity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a4c9-dd7d29e29976" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a\circ b = b\circ a</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-991a-c5d87f7f4039" class=""><strong>412. Associativity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-afcc-f7da76f72c80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(a\circ b)\circ c = a\circ (b\circ c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-9090-c401fe5d9549" class=""><strong>413. Idempotence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-8b41-d220d92ba438" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a\circ a=a</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-958b-c94ecf226068" class=""><strong>414. Convergence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8ace-f619bdb59113" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
state_i \to state^\* \ \forall i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-b779-f78cd48779ad" class=""><strong>415. Monotone join-semilattice</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-9a79-ee1f80f1b647" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\sqsubseteq x\sqcup y</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b603-cd7cf9270b0d" class=""><strong>416. Inflationary updates</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a4f5-de53f302bc88" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x&#x27; = update(x)\Rightarrow x \sqsubseteq x&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-978b-e093f404508f" class=""><strong>417. Join yields least upper bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-90c9-e9c7869c3d59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\sqsubseteq z \land y\sqsubseteq z \Rightarrow x\sqcup y \sqsubseteq z</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8edc-ed6d2f835d47" class=""><strong>418. Merge determinism</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-b6d4-d11ddec498a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
merge(x,y)=merge(x,y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a24f-c683f004ba86" class=""><strong>419. Delta-CRDT equivalence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-ad40-fd7fe0556840" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
apply(x,\delta)=merge(x,\delta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-ae01-e98c3b2b1e6c" class=""><strong>420. tombstone_{t+1}\supseteq tombstone_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-b4cb-f4cc8819d0d7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9828-fecfdf68212b" class="">C) Event sourcing and auditability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a47b-e6dda32a5519" class=""><strong>421. e(t_2)=e(t_1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-97fe-fc39bf141b2f" class=""><strong>422. Event append-only stream</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-84ad-f428f2fb9b0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1}=E_t\ \|\ e</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a214-cda3603f3479" class=""><strong>423. Sequence strict monotonicity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-beb1-f5305302d155" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
seq_{i+1}=seq_i+1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-bf40-cb474702cac1" class=""><strong>424. No gaps in sequence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-9ef6-ca04fccf059f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall k:\ exists\ e:\ seq(e)=k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-955e-fed7f8017f63" class=""><strong>425. fold(apply,s_0,E)=fold(apply,s_0,E)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a99e-f6a8c1348206" class=""><strong>426. Snapshot equivalence</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a816-fc7b8ca33bda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
fold(s_0,E)=fold(s_k,E_{k+1..n})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8284-e9860cbfa336" class=""><strong>427. Exactly-one projection update per event</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-af26-dfdb68a23c41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
count(update(projection,e))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-9660-ff3922b2d57a" class=""><strong>428. Projection eventually catches up</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b3c3-c9a0cc3c862c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Box \Diamond (proj\_seq = stream\_seq)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-914f-d4d29b56e58b" class=""><strong>429. Audit provenance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8c19-d6ac7174451d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
prov(change)=event\_id</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9aaa-eac6bc9d1719" class=""><strong>430. Replay produces same outputs</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-a74f-e1ca2a681246" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
outputs(replay(E))=outputs(replay(E))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9332-ff105de03250"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a5aa-d13ca25743a7" class="">D) Databases, isolation, and anomalies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-ab77-c6c659e9447c" class=""><strong>431. No dirty read</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-ad68-de718b81d943" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
read(T_2)\ \text{cannot see uncommitted}(T_1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a8b5-ed63e21c93fa" class=""><strong>432. No non-repeatable read (if promised)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-b140-ed338379fbee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
read(T,k,t_1)=v \Rightarrow read(T,k,t_2)=v</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-8f88-fa7d95589f39" class=""><strong>433. No phantom reads (if promised)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a9f7-cf872c278dde" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
result(Q,t_1)=result(Q,t_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b878-c0ef485d4122" class=""><strong>434. Serializable schedule</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-b54e-c16dea1dce53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H \equiv S</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-88ca-f2aa66d049ae" class=""><strong>435. Write skew forbidden</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-b16a-e8ddc9a83ef0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
constraint\ holds \Rightarrow \text{after concurrent commits constraint holds}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8d87-cf061997e370" class=""><strong>436. Unique index enforcement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-bd42-c34b37c10b15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
insert(k)\Rightarrow \neg exists(k)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-ae7f-fe0d0de06653" class=""><strong>437. Check constraint preservation</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-8357-fdaf2ecc5acc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
valid(row)\Rightarrow valid(update(row))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a7c2-f835308f7d12" class=""><strong>438. Read-your-writes</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-8997-cbd43e4e7e2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
write(T,k,v)\Rightarrow read(T,k)=v</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9842-c4f2e92d1af0" class=""><strong>439. Monotone primary key</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a5fb-c4760fcb50cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
pk_{i+1} &gt; pk_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b366-dab680eb2b16" class=""><strong>440. Durable commit</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-9f10-c4bb887178bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
commit(T)\Rightarrow persisted(T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-8caa-cc10b7ca7597"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-81c4-ffff7f4bbb02" class="">E) Performance and complexity guarantees</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-aea1-c59ac8540081" class=""><strong>441. P99 latency bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-8f49-d2aa29de5262" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(L \le L_{p99})\ge 0.99</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a2ea-c97221f30bf6" class=""><strong>442. Throughput lower bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-87e4-e7dde0a5af24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
throughput \ge \tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9e01-d0f1d6b003a2" class=""><strong>443. Bounded amplification</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-b445-fa66ecaa13cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
work\_internal \le \alpha \cdot work\_external</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9b2c-c22ba3ab2ab2" class=""><strong>444. Bounded lock contention</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-a8db-ee93b124e5fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
