class=""><strong>171B</strong>: T_next = T 
 δ1·Control</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-bcbd-db951d011c4c" class=""><strong>172A</strong>: Shadow = h0 + h1·Control</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8e3f-d354d76a6fe5" class=""><strong>172B</strong>: Transparency = tr0 − tr1·Shadow</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-915e-ecd96c704224" class=""><strong>173A</strong>: Transparency = tr0 − tr1·Shadow</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a7c6-ffe65e9516a7" class=""><strong>173B</strong>: Rumor = rm0 + rm1·(1−Transparency)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-addd-d11aea5b9efb" class=""><strong>174A</strong>: Rumor = rm0 + rm1·(1−Transparency)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8b00-d4c09fe3e96a" class=""><strong>174B</strong>: Volatility = v0 + v2·Rumor</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a64c-d072eae7c022" class=""><strong>175A</strong>: Volatility = v0 + v2·Rumor</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-a4ce-e81a5cf15607" class=""><strong>175B</strong>: Risk_premium = rp0 + rp2·Volatility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-993f-cc6aab0b80cb" class=""><strong>176A</strong>: Risk_premium = rp0 + rp2·Volatility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-902f-e61456a6809f" class=""><strong>176B</strong>: Investment = I0 − i2·Risk_premium</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-b99a-cae45d2c7aa0" class=""><strong>177A</strong>: Investment = I0 − i2·Risk_premium</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-a66d-eef2d0464c2f" class=""><strong>177B</strong>: Growth = g0 + g1·Investment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-a96d-ceeac6afaf88" class=""><strong>178A</strong>: Growth = g0 + g1·Investment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bc7a-ddf1eeddd19d" class=""><strong>178B</strong>: T = T0 + t1·Growth</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b986-e3f2c3c7256a" class=""><strong>179A</strong>: (T↓) ⇒ Control↑ (định nghĩa 171A)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-ba46-d7f7a06641d2" class=""><strong>179B</strong>: (Control↑) ⇒ Shadow↑ (định nghĩa 172A)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8cf8-ca089065aae9" class=""><strong>180A</strong>: Loop_strength = c1·h1·tr1·rm1·v2·rp2·i2</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-bb28-f339ba484714" class=""><strong>180B</strong>: If Loop_strength &gt; 
1 ⇒ hệ tự khuếch đại suy giảm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9723-c0b3936826b3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-9dd4-c87d5cf3ebff" class="">181–190 (Invariant sâu)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a16b-d007bd8cad66" class=""><strong>181A</strong>: Informal_agreement_weight = 1 − Formal_enforcement</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-b801-e116629781ee" class=""><strong>181B</strong>: System_predictability = sp0 − sp1·Informal_agreement_weight</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-946e-cdb2db70a2b0" class=""><strong>182A</strong>: Stability = 1 if “no one pushes limits” else 0</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-b7ad-de1be4441ee3" class=""><strong>182B</strong>: Hidden_risk = hr0 + hr1·Stability (khi Stability=1 nhưng không sửa cấu trúc)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8ae1-e797a4f2f391" class=""><strong>183A</strong>: Legal_knowledge_silence = σ( a21·Speech_cost )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-99ba-ec90502ce5b1" class=""><strong>183B</strong>: Error_visibility = ev0 − ev1·Legal_knowledge_silence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-809c-ec5ed3dc2a86" class=""><strong>184A</strong>: Parallel_ops = σ( a22·F + a23·(1−T) )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-945a-d80a955d7d5c" class=""><strong>184B</strong>: Formal_system_use = fs0 − fs1·Parallel_ops</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-a017-ecd25d241d26" class=""><strong>185A</strong>: Asset_diversification_out = σ( a24·Risk_perception )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bf80-ef94bc15a253" class=""><strong>185B</strong>: D
omestic_capital_depth = cd0 − cd1·Asset_diversification_out</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a241-e7c609ba99b1" class=""><strong>186A</strong>: Middle_defense = σ( a25·Income_volatility + a26·Healthcare_risk )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-8ca1-f762ba26f33b" class=""><strong>186B</strong>: Consumption_growth = cg0 − cg1·Middle_defense</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b206-c02d1c71ac03" class=""><strong>187A</strong>: Poor_shadow_dependence = σ( a27·F + a28·Access_cost )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-b46c-eec56f78b761" class=""><strong>187B</strong>: Equality = eq0 − eq1·Poor_shadow_dependence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-ba2c-f015382f5adb" class=""><strong>188A</strong>: Elite_network_reliance = σ( a29·E + a30·(1−T) )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9149-d25527e0f0ed" class=""><strong>188B</strong>: Rule_of_law = rol0 − rol1·Elite_network_reliance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-b591-d8c1f4bf9d34" class=""><strong>189A</strong>: Loyalty_preference = lp0 − lp1·Opportunity_uncertainty</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-ba5d-cc5ced5cf130" class=""><strong>189B</strong>: Org_cohesion = oc0 + oc1·Loyalty_preference</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-8d6c-ff0be12965b6" class=""><strong>190A</strong>: Value_negotiability = vn0 + vn1·(1−T)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-ae5a-cb61bb214cb7" class=""><strong>190B</strong>: Moral_hazard = mh0 + mh1·Value_negotiability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-8ba2-d2f82a199d06"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-ad46-ed7bcf2ef944" class="">191–200 (Invariant sâu tiếp)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a0e1-f9fe397ebf19" class=""><strong>191A</strong>: Formality_inflation = σ( a31·Weak_delivery )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-a4cb-da45481485b2" class=""><strong>191B</strong>: Gap = Gap0 + g3·Formality_inflation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b35a-e61aff3aa8b3" class=""><strong>192A</strong>: Damping = dp0 − dp1·C (ambient chaos)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-ab6f-c81c8c181479" class=""><strong>192B</strong>: Gain = g0 + g4·(1−Damping)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9bf6-d5d764bdd384" class=""><strong>193A</strong>: Info_vacuum = 1 − Transparency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b6d1-ef6a73627547" class=""><strong>193B</strong>: Rumor = rm0 + rm2·Info_vacuum</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-9c2b-f1945b80e70b" class=""><strong>194A</strong>: Crackdown_without_reform = 1 if enforcement_spike &amp; 
reform=0 else 0</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b5d7-ca82a2935122" class=""><strong>194B</strong>: Long_run_T = T − δ2·Crackdown_without_reform</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-b662-e73b6ab6b11f" class=""><strong>195A</strong>: Rule_enforcement_ratio = Enforcement / Rule_density</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8366-e0844c8cf27b" class=""><strong>195B</strong>: T = T0 + t2·Rule_enforcement_ratio</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-906f-cf6173865a73" class=""><strong>196A</strong>: Personal_safe_zone = σ( a32·(1−S) + a33·(1−T) )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-8053-d12f37aaefc2" class=""><strong>196B</strong>: Public_good_care = pg0 − pg1·Personal_safe_zone</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-accd-cfbeb52fa424" class=""><strong>197A</strong>: Responsibility_fear = σ( a34·Blame_cost )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-9ace-d8386c6f3e29" class=""><strong>197B</strong>: K = K0 − k5·Responsibility_fear</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a2aa-e08ced62a6f3" class=""><strong>198A</strong>: Network_gatekeeping = σ( a35·Rent_seeking )</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-909f-f2991578b658" class=""><strong>198B</strong>: Meritocracy = m0 − m2·Network_gatekeeping</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-81ad-da284bce1505" class=""><strong>199A</strong>: Hidden_risk_stock = ∑(Unresolved_failures)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-94ba-e4b98fa197eb" class=""><strong>199B</strong>: Shock_sensitivity = ss0 + ss1·Hidden_risk_stock</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-8799-ee9de7ff47fb" c
lass=""><strong>200A</strong>: Cooperation_cost = cc0 + cc1·F + cc2·Suspicion_baseline</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-854a-f7ae31d5bc86" class=""><strong>200B</strong>: If Cooperation_cost &gt; Cooperation_value ⇒ System_output ↓</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-82a7-d76ec6ec1560"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-ace6-c2446155ab04" class="">Nếu bạn nói <strong>Next</strong>, tôi sẽ làm thêm theo đúng phong cách này nhưng <strong>đổi tập tín hiệu</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-b58f-c6e92f06b556" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">1–100 (tầng đời sống &amp; doanh nghiệp phổ thông) → cũng 200 micro-equations (mỗi tín hiệu 2 eq).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-97d6-c2d1a6377f3a" class="">Dưới đây là <strong>bộ phương trình (equations) gắn trực tiếp</strong> vào 100 tín hiệu vừa rồi (101–200). 
Không cần dữ liệu thống kê; 
chỉ cần <strong>chấm điểm quan sát</strong> (0–1 hoặc đếm tần suất) để suy ra biến hệ.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-85ee-f24d9968853c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-bc19-f91973c84cec" class="">1) Các biến quan sát (0–1) để “cắm” vào phương trình</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a606-cf37127eee66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Q</strong> = Queue integrity (kỷ luật xếp hàng)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-a281-c6e7c8a328bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>U</strong> = Unobserved compliance (tuân thủ khi không bị quan sát)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-98b0-c8b7bb46ad03" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>O</strong> = Observed compliance (tuân thủ khi có lực lượng/giám sát)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-abe6-f363c74e17d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>N</strong> = Network dependence (phụ thuộc quan hệ)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-a891-fb8d04d30040" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>F</strong> = Friction (ma sát thủ tục)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-8dd9-d48170162d3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>E</strong> = Enforcement selectivity (thực thi chọn lọc)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-81af-d01fe78a7820" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>A</strong> = Ambient chaos (hỗn loạn nền: ồn, chen lấn, 
lấn chiếm)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b309-e94f3e464237" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>P</strong> = Signaling intensity (hình thức/PR/“đẹp báo cáo”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a31e-e1da307fbfa9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>D</strong> = Delivery (thực chất: hoàn thành, sửa lỗi, vận hành)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-8add-c6ea69d127cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>H</strong> = Shadow activity (hệ ngầm: “lo trọn gói”, “có cách”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8e7f-ff6877c2b52f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>X</strong> = Exit intent (ý định thoát: visa, tài sản ngoại biên)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-912e-c6a11f61eee5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>S</strong> = Social safety (cảm giác an toàn)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-af39-d3a7789292e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>K</strong> = Execution capacity (năng lực triển khai)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-ac89-d3d3b59256db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R</strong> = Defensive behavior (hành vi phòng thủ: tích tiền, 
né rủi ro)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-880c-ff84febf7cca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>M</strong> = Maintenance ratio (bảo trì chủ động / chắp vá)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-ba43-fdd664808d3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-b537-c3f61cad4b56" class="">2) Phương trình lõi: Niềm tin thể chế, ma sát, hệ ngầm</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-90f9-c21eba206f8b" class="">(1) Niềm tin thể chế <strong>T</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a70e-ea2a637bc2de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \approx \frac{U}{O}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a6f7-e038417ecbc5" class="">(2) Niềm tin qua xếp hàng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8d75-f58390096593" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \propto Q</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-818b-fdab0074ccff" class="">(3) Ma sát hành chính <strong>F</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8c25-e41cee445845" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F = \alpha_1 \cdot Var(req) + \alpha_2 \cdot Layers + \alpha_3 \cdot Time</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-a280-ff94e7655e47" class="">(4) Hệ ngầm <strong>H</strong> nở khi ma sát cao và niềm tin thấp</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bb94-e7678fa6c75c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H = \sigma(\beta_1 F + \beta_2 (1-T))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-8f6d-cc2eaf0c0463" class="">(5) Thực thi chọn lọc làm niềm tin giảm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-9ab5-ef7d43522edb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T = T_0 - \gamma E</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-999d-f9126b80e8cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9dd2-fc4b95751307" class="">3) Phương trình “hình thức–thực chất” (rất đặc thù C6 muộn)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-a7d9-e8373f34ed65" class="">(6) Độ lệch hình thức–thực chất <strong>Gap</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9d07-ef95d340524e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gap = P - D</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-b1a2-c10e0ad548c4" class="">(7) Gap kéo niềm tin xuống và tăng hệ ngầm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-ab5e-e2eede4ce21a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T = T_0 - \lambda Gap</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-bca0-c0843db74ee3" class="">H = H_0 + \mu Gap<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-97f8-e1d3f0928ae8" class="">(8) Năng lực triển khai <strong>K</strong> giảm khi Gap tăng và sợ trách nhiệm tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-8b03-f4d9030483f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K = K_0 - \eta_1 Gap - \eta_2 Fear(blame)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-aeba-f1e05f06f9dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a333-c886a3cf66b2" class="">4) Vòng lặp “kiểm soát–hệ ngầm” (nghịch lý kiểm soát)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-9bb0-e7440ba7df49" class="">(9) Khi niềm tin giảm, kiểm soát tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-9bf7-c76c563be396" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Control = c_0 + c_1(1-T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-bc32-d3a761cdf86e" class="">(10) Kiểm soát tăng → hệ ngầm tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-950a-f20b79c26a89" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H = h_0 + h_1 Control</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-8989-fe930e8ba058" class="">(11) Hệ ngầm tăng → minh bạch giảm → niềm tin giảm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-be03-dfac353b7567" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Transparency = \tau_0 - \tau_1 H</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-832b-f48512e237d7" class="">T = T_0 + \tau_2 Transparency<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9c47-cf8051e37df8" class="">Gộp thành vòng:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-aead-c1962d4a9e0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \downarrow \Rightarrow Control \uparrow \Rightarrow H \uparrow \Rightarrow Transparency \downarrow \Rightarrow T \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-bd78-dd96a6278cd5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-90d0-ec5ba39f2bf8" class="">5) “Hỗn loạn nền” và an toàn xã hội</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8055-cfa0e4cf57f7" class="">(12) An toàn xã hội <strong>S</strong> giảm khi hỗn loạn nền <strong>A</strong> tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-afb6-d07526fd2f6e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S = S_0 - \kappa A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-ad64-c2cf2e13475c" class="">(13) Phòng vệ <strong>R</strong> tăng khi S giảm và T giảm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-92d3-f48b0f9018da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R = r_0 + r_1(1-S) + r_2(1-T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-97ba-d48ee1204d5f" class="">(14) Gain phản ứng xã hội <strong>G</strong> (dễ bùng tin đồn)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-960a-d993941aa215" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G = \frac{\Delta Rumor}{\Delta Official\_clarification}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a66a-c11c82f3bd0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G \propto \frac{1}{Transparency}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b75c-fe6854bfa721"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b136-c20b26bcc18e" class="">6) Exit (thoát ra) là hệ quả của (T, F, S)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-9bfd-e2b5acda3309" class="">(15) Ý định thoát <strong>X</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b5ba-e904dc45f8d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X = \phi_1(1-T) + \phi_2 F + \phi_3(1-S)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b535-c2a1a5c60c6d" class="">(16) X tăng → đầu tư dài hạn giảm</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-957a-d9dddef4304f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
LongTermInvestment = I_0 - \psi X</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-be53-fde2ce3e3417"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-af2b-e4a1cfccd8ca" class="">7) Hạ tầng: bảo trì vs chắp vá (không cần số liệu, chỉ cần quan sát)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-aa1f-ef986959fdcf" class="">(17) Chỉ số bảo trì <strong>M</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-81a5-c007a3269a20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M = \frac{PreventiveMaintenance}{PatchFix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-adee-dca4283d37ef" class="">(18) Khi M thấp → sự cố tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8984-fb1ee9d3f283" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BreakdownRate \propto \frac{1}{M}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a7c1-fdb44ac15050" class="">(19) Khi sự cố tăng → S giảm và R tăng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8f5f-d1acd9cdcbb6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S = S_0 - \omega BreakdownRate</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-8854-d4dab0f478bb" class="">R = R_0 + \xi BreakdownRate<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8380-db05302dc457"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8627-c76a211fb3ec" class="">8) “Điểm gãy” (tipping point) dạng điều kiện</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-b45b-c99273676ed5" class="">(20) Điều kiện hệ bắt đầu tự suy thoái nhanh (C6→C6 muộn)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-ac1a-c9978fbfab2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gap &gt; Gap^\* \ \land \ T &lt; T^\* \ \land \ H &gt; H^\*</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-bc0d-e0eab9fa709d" class="">(21) Điều kiện chuyển sang tái ổn định thật (C7 islands)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-9d00-f9e1ed0d8571" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D &gt; 
P \ \land \ E \downarrow \ \land \ F \downarrow \ \land \ Transparency \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-9aa1-f4ec9e0708a2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-a0bb-e78b5376a34d" class="">9) Gắn nhanh vào nhóm 101–200 (mapping 1 dòng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-ac9e-f67179b08f3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Elite phòng vệ</strong> (101–110): , 
,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b520-d4069b75f613" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Pháp lý chọn lọc</strong> (111–120):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9eb3-faca984a77bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DNNN hình thức</strong> (121–130):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-ab4a-eaf4a66abc0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hạ tầng/điện</strong> (131–140):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-bd25-f888c735e3ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Giáo dục tín hiệu</strong> (141–150):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-bdbd-edc517e15f18" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tiêu dùng vị thế</strong> (151–160):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-b42d-e564dfe55d2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tâm lý xã hội</strong> (161–170):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b9ea-c3b243727437" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meta vòng lặp</strong> (171–200): vòng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b08a-e3f2e57ef60d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-92df-f67f9e635a22" class="">Nếu bạn nói <strong>Next</strong>, tôi sẽ viết tiếp <strong>200 phương trình “micro”</strong> (mỗi tín hiệu 1 equation riêng, không dùng chung), 
nhưng sẽ dài và dạng checklist đúng theo kiểu bạn đang ép độ sâu.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a0d3-d2398622107e" class="">Dưới đây là <strong>100 phương trình lõi (Core Equations 1–100)</strong> cho mô hình cấu trúc văn hoá ẩn Việt.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-bcf2-ccbbdf0d695a" class="">Ký hiệu: σ(.) là hàm chuẩn hoá (0–1). Tất cả biến có thể chấm 0–1/0–2.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-b4f0-c04e6f17f0a3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9382-deb501ac78eb" class="">A. 
TÁCH LỚP &amp; 
ẨN TÍNH</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-945c-f621f027c269" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>ΔLayer = RP − SP</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b3ea-ede02f07bb56" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>HN = σ(ΔLayer + BC + TT − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-be99-e174c41c72d7" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Layer Stability LS = 1 − |ΔLayer|</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-8689-d04362074590" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Hidden Load HL = BC · TT</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-bb61-c7d4466f6e53" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Narrative Gap NG = SP − Observed Behavior (OB)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-91b1-cb1eba27fb87" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Authenticity Index AI = 1 − |NG|</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8595-fe3728006ff4" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Shadow Authority SA = RP · (1 − Formal Role Strength FRS)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-9bf0-c23856ef8ffd" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Public Coherence PC = SP · OB</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-98d6-ce51141c2b1d" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Signal Compression SC = Context Sensitivity (CS) − Explicit Contracting (EC)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-ac94-f80086b413e9" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Transparency Tₚ = A · (1 − TT)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a695-f3e75b4866a3"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-89ba-ff72703a2aed" class="">B. 
QUYỀN LỰC “CỬA” &amp; 
MẠNG</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a7f6-fc04886f4b06" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>GP = αQ + βN − γRS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-82e6-e8fc84812f01" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>O = GP · (1 − RS)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-944f-da1df13d4aae" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Access Inequality AIₙ = Var(Q across groups)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-89f6-d56caa479dcb" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Network Leverage NL = N · (1 − RS)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-9304-f06e46ef90fb" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Gate Concentration GC = Σ(top 10% Q)/ΣQ</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9287-f3b964969a5c" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Informal Influence II = BC · N</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-88b8-ecc09b280cc0" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Decision Centrality DC = Corr(Decisions, 
N)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8cec-db3f4505356f" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Entry Barrier EB = GP · SRC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-90cc-f6acbf6b9e47" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Merit Weight MW = C · RS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-b954-fe84e8d262c5" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Network Bias NB = (1 − RS) · N</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-9bec-d61d1a3c2a0a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-8cf7-d47d899c5118" class="">C. 
MẬP MỜ &amp; SỰ THẬT</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-9ace-f77f09d0bf88" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>K = f(D)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9c70-f1249c256197" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Aᵦ = σ(K − T)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-86fa-fd3736db06fd" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>PT = T · (1 − Aᵦ)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-bcd1-cf16b6afdab2" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Truth Drift TD = TT · (1 − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-873f-e71e7ebac807" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Leak Pressure LP = LR − A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9445-c74a9ab0aab3" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Selective Disclosure SD = TT · IG</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-aed5-e00b09cdb626" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Public Candor PCa = A − TT</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-aad9-ea3ffb6a1e70" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Risk-Adjusted Truth RAT = PT − SRC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-8d48-cbfe6af9c289" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Narrative Control NC = BC + TT</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-9c7f-f82a1dca8343" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Trust TR = (1 − TT) · A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-bc67-e1232307b264"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-b33b-df0adbbb246b" class="">D. 
FACE vs LIÊM SỈ</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-b911-f487ab51b436" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>FD = σ(IC − MC)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8d96-da95dd569c05" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>ER = CA − FD</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a0a1-fb58d7b07e07" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Shame Alignment S = 1 − FD</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-9e6f-e08cd0e337d7" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Image Premium IPₙ = IC − A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8e7c-e306cd810b8e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Moral Yield MY = CA · A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b92d-ce7e6a04a5ba" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Cosmetic Repair CR = FD · (1 − Structural Change SCₕ)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-ac3f-ce48836b4631" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Accountability Deficit AD = 1 − A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-a4ce-e8816b4e0588" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Face Cost FC = SRC · FD</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-af70-f238cbc1d747" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Ethical Threshold ET = CA − SRC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b75b-f7012e2d40ae" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Face Regime FR = FD · (1 − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-b64c-f721df473c31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-9a72-d442375619c1" class="">E. 
PHÂN BỔ &amp; 
LỆCH CHUẨN</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-bbcb-c9bd0069c3b7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>π = σ(NB − RA) + σ(C · RA)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a2ab-d1d10925e10f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>RD = NB · (1 − RA)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-801e-c4b84876e00d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Allocation Fairness AF = RA · (1 − NB)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-bf1e-e5593cb0accd" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Favoritism Fv = NB · IG</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8f42-e9f4221e595d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Meritocracy M = C · RS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-bf2a-d07faa92d317" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Discretionary Power DP = 1 − RA</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8dcc-e20528d972d7" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Resource Capture RC = NB · DP</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-a8a3-cf0c0da27a07" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Budget Opacity BO = 1 − RA</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-9878-cbf84e34a38f" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Opportunity Gap OG = Var(O across groups)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b079-e8099bc43493" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Performance Signal PSf = C − NB</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9227-e3c21d9750bd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-82b3-e9519defa9bc" c
lass="">F. 
BẢO VỆ &amp; 
TRÁCH NHIỆM</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-b74f-ca0a180387ee" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>PA = σ(IG + ST − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-9b41-ea27ed694fc4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>DH = PA · (1 − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-9d7a-cd509630b7ee" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Scapegoat Probability SPg = (1 − IG) · (1 − A)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-b7e1-c43a16dc8d2a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Protection Bias PB = IG − A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8818-c06d5253acb9" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Crisis Integrity CIₖ = A · CA</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-9160-c481dc4e6024" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Blame Diffusion BD = 1 − Individual Accountability IA</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-8c7e-e848d29c4749" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Structural Fix SF = A · Structural Change SCₕ</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-a32c-e6db6e031452" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Incident Recurrence IRc = (1 − SF)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-93fa-d41447911966" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Protective Inequality PI = Var(PA)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-9428-d8eefb6f60fd" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Justice Index JI = A · (1 − PB)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a631-efd94c5092cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-991f-f38b999e66fb" class="">G. 
BACKCHANNEL &amp; 
HỢP THỨC</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-85ea-cc607f1daa7a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>P(Pre-decided) = σ(BC − F)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-a885-f26225b66757" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>MI = 1 − P(Pre-decided)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-aa17-dad57223b068" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Ritualization Rz = F · (1 − MI)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-9358-edf43cccc809" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Informal Dominance ID = BC · (1 − RS)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-abae-f6a1eada4d11" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Trace Avoidance TA = 1 − Documentation Dₒ</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-b6af-c526fe280421" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Meeting Authenticity MA = MI · A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-88e3-eed37bffa509" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Decision Latency DL = Timing Control TC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-99cc-f96e10041de0" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Timing Power TP = TC · SRC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-83c7-d92ee5744952" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Agenda Control ACₐ = Agenda Setter AS · BC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8c0f-f373f284627f" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Veto Power VP = SA + AS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9a43-cefec71bfe3c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-bfc9-fbcdaaa928b4" class="">H. 
PLACEMENT &amp; 
THỨ BẬC</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-9c20-c338a97f173e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>PS = P · H</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-b531-f251d7d29a87" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>SS = ΣPS − ΔRole</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-b35d-fca6894647cd" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Hierarchy Rigidity HR = H · (1 − Mobility)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a74a-fa6c53295865" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Status Mobility SM = 1 − HR</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8a6e-e138a5439d62" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Placement Inequality PIₙ = Var(P)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-b9f5-c72295214516" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Symbolic Capital SCa = P · IC</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a434-e538bd472042" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Voice Share VS = Speaking Time / Total</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bc13-da8dfb52002e" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Silence Power SPw = 1 − VS (for high-status)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-90a2-fcb2b0f9a82c" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Recognition Gap RG = Expected Credit − Actual Credit</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-ad02-dc5887cb8281" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Status Drift SDf = ΔRole over time</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-a91b-d7b03b037c92"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a3b9-d3957c76a245" class="">I. 
TRUYỀN CHUẨN &amp; 
ỔN ĐỊNH</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8b62-ccf5540eb693" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>CS = (FT + IR) − TG</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a972-e59782d4f8b6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>V = 1/CS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-8909-d330d622dbe1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Norm Retention NR = FT · (1 − TG)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8909-d6ab18ee2be3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Institutional Anchor IAₙ = IR · RS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-85c2-da89bda9a386" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Cultural Erosion CE = TG − (FT + IR)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b766-cb3e8b7f33e0" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Compliance Rate CRₙ = R · L</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-90f5-ee9c428f965b" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Moral Persistence MP = A · CA</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-bd6f-e156ecf8f94f" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Market Pressure MPₙ = MR − IR</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-89a2-ffb218c66007" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Standard Fragmentation SFg = V · MPₙ</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b241-ea31ef38e188" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Stability Preference SPf = SS · D</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-9729-cd561a67f335"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a455-c70060458425" class="">J. 
TỔNG HỢP &amp; 
CHẨN ĐOÁN</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-a9e4-c5b0fa9e6ac5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>HMS = w1·ΔLayer + w2·GP + w3·TT + w4·NB − w5·A</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8a7a-f00165185402" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>CI = R · L · A · M · S</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b4ef-e7c4cf80b54e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>SO = R · L</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-be49-cf2570c6f88b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>DE = A · M · S</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b028-de7313a6f927" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>RU = σ(BC + GP + TT − A − R)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-8833-d215518591c4" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>SOₚ = σ(A + R − BC − TT)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9f28-fd1ca029aded" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Fairness Perception FP = AF · JI</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-94ba-e958cbccde5d" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Long-term Trust LT = TR · CI</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-9393-d66ad5285733" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Collapse Risk CRₛ = HN + RD + DH − CI</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-97f0-d41fddf11149" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>System Integrity SI = CI − HN − RD</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a41b-d2b34549eea7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-ab69-d47d3c3dd57b" class="">Nếu bạn m
uốn, tôi có thể:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9d6f-e4a6c2024fd0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Chuẩn hoá 100 phương trình này thành <strong>mô hình động theo thời gian (t → t+1)</strong> để xem khi Market Pressure tăng thì CI, HN, RU biến động thế nào.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-9bed-e6cbff0f6e87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hoặc rút gọn thành <strong>20 biến tối giản</strong> đủ tái tạo toàn bộ hệ (compressed master model).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-94d6-d49d8a0eb299" class="">Tiếp theo — <strong>100 tín hiệu bỏ sót sâu hơn (elite, pháp lý, hành vi hệ thống)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-9a1b-d60fbf10a242" class="">Không cần số liệu. 
Chỉ cần quan sát thực tế.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a22a-c102ad4b2ae1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9ef5-c08815f0a0d7" class="">101–110: Hành vi của tầng elite (rất khó giả)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b11d-ea1eea27af01" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Elite nói “ổn” nhưng <strong>chuyển tài sản sang cấu trúc phòng vệ</strong>.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b968-dc91bf98c738" class="">Eq: Elite_defensive_structures ↑ ⇒ T_real ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-b4c2-f1be9bb5dc31" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Elite <strong>ít xuất hiện ngẫu nhiên</strong>, 
tăng kiểm soát môi trường tiếp xúc.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-b133-df0acffbd667" class="">Eq: Openness ↓ ⇒ S ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-842e-fc966cf92d09" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Dòng tiền elite chuyển sang <strong>tài sản ngoại biên</strong> (không công khai).<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b787-c7a5289ac85b" class="">Eq: Capital_externalization ↑ ⇒ X ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8981-cd0849a84ccb" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Con cái elite học ngành dễ di chuyển quốc tế.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-995b-efce0fe4f07e" class="">Eq: Education_portability ↑ ⇒ Exit_option ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-a880-f469693c065c" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Elite xây mạng riêng thay vì dựa vào thể chế.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-b347-de1a6d104704" class="">Eq: Private_network_strength ↑ ⇒ T ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-8338-cf26cb2fc96d" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Elite tránh đầu tư dài hạn nội địa ngoài ngành được bảo vệ.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8199-fdcae886eaea" class="">Eq: Risk_perception ↑ ⇒ Domestic_long_term_invest ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-be92-cccf30d9a053" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Thỏa thuận miệng có giá trị hơn hợp đồng.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-95eb-e99b6bf9512d" class="">Eq: Formal_enforcement ↓ ⇒ Informal_power ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-b7f6-d5c43189b6d9" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Elite ưu tiên “an toàn pháp lý cá nhân” hơn tăng trưởng.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-8aaf-e3f3fbb96d51" class="">Eq: Personal_risk ↑ ⇒ Innovation ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9faa-c72299c65cb9" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Xuất hiện “người gánh thay” trong cấu trúc trách nhiệm.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8381-c1e777c1b0a9" class="">Eq: Accountability_diffusion ↑ ⇒ System_risk ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-be63-c88b79d7df13" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Thay đổi nhân sự cấp cao nhưng cấu trúc không đổi.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-9990-f0ea34d24368" class="">Eq: Personnel_change ≠ Structural_change</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-bbf3-ef4a725cfec3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-8546-cee579843b6b" class="">111–120: Pháp lý và thanh lọc</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b5bc-e0fed2fdc5bc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Bắt lớn xảy ra theo chu kỳ, không liên tục.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a647-c3564159ece1" class="">Eq: Enforcement_spike ↑ &amp; 
Baseline_enforcement ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a7a0-da36c9b9ee37" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Sau thanh lọc, cơ chế không minh bạch hơn.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-aa5a-f74db8be7840" class="">Eq: Post_crackdown_transparency ≈ constant</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b1c3-ef29f6c6dbcc" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Người trong hệ sợ sai hơn sợ vi phạm.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9857-c2e5457395e7" class="">Eq: Fear ↑ ⇒ Paralysis ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9f88-e3eb9006507d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Quy định tăng nhưng hướng dẫn mơ hồ.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a499-ff30bfc5402a" class="">Eq: Rule_density ↑ &amp; 
Clarity ↓ ⇒ F ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-ace7-f7f8b455d08b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Án lệ không tạo tiền lệ ổn định.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8539-edf97c5cd233" class="">Eq: Legal_predictability ↓ ⇒ Investment ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a5ff-efa7a7e9108f" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Tăng xử phạt hành chính nhỏ lẻ.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-9f80-d56e84445e53" class="">Eq: Fine_frequency ↑ ⇒ Revenue_dependence ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-abf2-c1b9e8a7a484" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Hệ pháp lý phản ứng hơn là phòng ngừa.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-a116-fbc5beaf2396" class="">Eq: Prevention ↓ ⇒ Crisis_frequency ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-aa32-f152b640b3bf" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Không có cơ chế bảo vệ người tố cáo hiệu quả.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-95a3-fc3038ee9d88" class="">Eq: Whistleblower_safety ↓ ⇒ Hidden_risk ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-8cc0-de0d66138d52" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Doanh nghiệp lớn có “vùng an toàn mềm”.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-947a-ed18a21d57e5" class="">Eq: Enforcement_selectivity ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-97d2-febab6c6ac10" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Người dân tránh kiện tụng vì chi phí vô hình.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9686-da25fc33dc0a" class="">Eq: L
egal_accessibility ↓ ⇒ T ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-b2c7-ecce226b7930"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-86b3-e44178ebe4d1" class="">121–130: Doanh nghiệp nhà nước / bán nhà nước</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-939f-f1bca7187226" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Quy mô lớn nhưng biên lợi nhuận thấp.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b671-dd15122b0adb" class="">Eq: Scale ↑ &amp; Efficiency ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-97bf-fd75c5cffe8a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Lợi nhuận phụ thuộc chính sách.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bcb5-ece64de341f4" class="">Eq: Policy_dependency ↑ ⇒ Market_competitiveness ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-9495-daa7c2bcf9c4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Đầu tư dàn trải ngoài lõi.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b51c-dd6daa7e7460" class="">Eq: Focus ↓ ⇒ Risk ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-ab26-fdc186ad5701" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Báo cáo lợi nhuận nhưng dòng tiền căng.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-8c21-caf108504436" class="">Eq: Profit ≠ Cash_flow</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-8aeb-fddbe5a2cc4d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Thay đổi lãnh đạo nhưng dự án dở dang không giảm.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-872f-c5ea4e65b937" class="">Eq: Governance_rotation ↑ &amp; 
Project_completion ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9aa9-c4fbc2d5a688" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Tập trung hình ảnh thương hiệu quốc gia hơn hiệu quả vận hành.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-b1a6-fe02ca9af6d2" class="">Eq: P ↑ &amp; D ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-8839-da87a2cb61a7" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Đòn bẩy cao trong chu kỳ tăng trưởng.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a78e-d4d536474f83" class="">Eq: Leverage ↑ ⇒ Collapse_sensitivity ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-a93c-c31ba933d2f5" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Hợp đồng chéo giữa nhóm thân hữu.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-a6b7-d09bf70ddd3f" class="">Eq: Network_density ↑ ⇒ Competition ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-bc1a-c13c0dd2c6fe" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Thị trường không phản ánh rủi ro thực.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-a29e-c72a20cbe423" class="">Eq: Price_signal_distortion ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-93ef-f6856ec9c222" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Hệ phụ thuộc vào “giữ ổn định” hơn tối ưu hóa.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8767-d06c5c47ea6d" class="">Eq: Stability_priority ↑ ⇒ Efficiency ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-9293-c6cc4f297288"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-957d-e9321a3acaa8" class="">131–140: Hệ năng lượng &amp;

 Allocation_efficiency ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-9cd1-e944a7de80ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9e54-df2ea8ac874b" class="">141–150: Tầng giáo dục &amp; nhân lực</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-b1eb-c43878e70ac1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Bằng cấp nhiều, kỹ năng thực thấp.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-ade4-f3dc7848477f" class="">Eq: Credential_density ↑ &amp; Competence ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-ac15-c860a974c410" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Học thuộc &gt;

style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-a08d-db5fae630730" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Hệ đào tạo không linh hoạt với thị trường.<div dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a837-d2eff6504608"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-8662-f441a89a3e4a" class="">151–160: Hành vi tiêu id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8889-e378480f79b3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Ưu t
iên hình ảnh hơn độ bền.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-83b9-deb00fe4053a" class="">Eq: P ↑ &amp; Durability ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b9a9-c78c8a6cef0f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Chu kỳ nâng cấp nhanh dù thu nhập thấp.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-aa33-db04b81507c9" class="">Eq: Consumption_pressure ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-94b5-cec26fcb21e1" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Mua để “giữ vị thế” xã hội.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b1f9-ca9767ab4266" class="">Eq: Status_signal ↑ ⇒ Savings ↓</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-99b7-c5a9a15c9dba" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Thanh toán chậm phổ biến.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b9af-f4e245aafa2f" class="">Eq: Liquidity_stress ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b776-f27ead8739c8" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Đầu tư vào đất nhiều hơn sản xuất.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-89c9-d6b0eac1182d" class="">Eq: Productive_capital ↓ &amp; 
Speculative_capital ↑</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-8541-eba1569724e4" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Thị trường tin đồn style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-92e1-d89f184afdb6" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Người dân ưu tiên tài sản cứng hơn tài sản tài chính.<div id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-8fc8-fdc23c924b16" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Tăng mua bảo hiểm không vì bảo vệ mà vì lo sợ.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-95f4-f74a415a966a" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Thích giải pháp nhanh hơn giải pháp bền.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-bc53-f4642298d1d4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Mệt mỏi tập thể.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p
